<script>
    function getCountryCode(){
        return 'SE';
    }
    function getLanguageCode(){
        return 'sv';
    }
    function getApiHost(){
        return 'https:\/\/api.flysas.com\/';
    }
	function getExternalizedRootPage(){
		return 'https:\/\/www.sas.se';
	}
	function getLocale(){
        return 'sv';
	}
	function getPOS() {
		var pos = getCountryCode();
		if(getCountryCode() == 'BLUEPRINT'){
			pos = 'SE';
		} else if (pos.length == 0 || pos.length > 2) {
        	pos = 'LU';
        }
        return pos;
    }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
  function read_cookie(key)
  {
      var result;
      return (result = new RegExp('(?:^|; )' + encodeURIComponent(key) + '=([^;]*)').exec(document.cookie)) ? (result[1]) : null;
  }
  var language = read_cookie('_language');
     if (language !== undefined) {
     document.getElementsByTagName('html')[0].setAttribute('lang', language);
     }
 </script>
